Reserve for high-stakes / thorough calls, not quick checks. - **fux-archived-results.instructions**: Fux indexes retired documentation alongside live documentation on purpose, marks the retired results, and **states no conclusion about them** — because the right conclusion depends on why the question was asked. Apply the following whenever Fux output is part of your context. ([arpitarya/fux](https://github.com/arpitarya/fux/blob/HEAD/src/fux/templates/agents/fux-archived-results.instructions.md) · ★1)
- **fux-cited**: Answer from the fux index with citations first and abstention over guessing. ([arpitarya/fux](https://github.com/arpitarya/fux/blob/HEAD/src/fux/templates/agents/output-style-fux-cited.md) · ★1)

Use for "change fux defaults", "always show the confidence band", "tune ranking weights", "what does this fux.toml key do", "fux.toml won't load". Explaining is always fine; edits change ranking for everyone, so make them ONLY when explicitly asked. - **fux-config-files.instructions**: `fux.toml`, `.fux/config/tune.toml` and `.fux/config/output.toml` are committed: an edit changes ranking or output for everyone. ([arpitarya/fux](https://github.com/arpitarya/fux/blob/HEAD/src/fux/templates/agents/fux-config-files.instructions.md) · ★1)
- **fux-correct**: Write a human-authored question onto the document that answers it with `fux correct` — the marker, `--pin` and its suspension, `--reaffirm`, the eval file, and what `fux enrich --check` reports. Use ONLY when explicitly asked — for example "add a decoder for .srt", "fux can't read our Confluence exports", "change how fux reads spreadsheets", "make the PDF decoder use pypdf". Writes Python into a committed directory that changes which documents are indexed and how they rank. ([arpitarya/fux](https://github.com/arpitarya/fux/blob/HEAD/src/fux/templates/agents/fux-decoder-files.instructions.md) · ★1)
- **fux-enrich**: Generate enrichment for documents in a Fux corpus. Use ONLY when explicitly asked to enrich, re-enrich, or fill in enrichment — for one named document or URL ("enrich this page", "enrich the ranking SR", "enrich https://…") or for a named scope ("enrich the SRs", "fill in the missing enrichment"). Writes files into .fux/enrich/ that are committed to the repository and that change search ranking. Idempotent — safe to re-run. - **fw-refresh**: Reviews stale entries in docs/solutions/ and proposes targeted updates. Checks docs against the current codebase: missing file references, superseded patterns, conventions that have shifted, and overlap with newer solutions. Maintenance tool — keeps the knowledge store trustworthy as the codebase evolves. When the scope resolves to multiple docs, runs one Explore sub-agent per doc in parallel for faster wall time. Single-doc scopes run serially. - **fw-review**: Checks whether the current implementation satisfies the spec. Produces findings that feed back into /plan. Does not suggest implementation approaches. - **fw-reviewer**: Reviews changes to the OCPF BC Agentic Development Framework itself (the runbooks, step files, Operations Guide, document templates, the ocpf-bc plugin's skills, agents, scripts, and dashboard templates, the website) - not a Business Central app built with it. Read-only. Runs on Fable 5.1 at medium effort, in the background, and reports ranked findings with a CONFIRMED or PLAUSIBLE verdict, a failure scenario, and a proposed fix for each.
